2026-10-19T06:09:32.8346313Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [profile] Serve the main image with the Prefill attention prologue, contiguous-KV FA3 extend and Prefill-only norm + quant applied as an SGLang overlay

Run 39198642036 (Prefill bundle image b8531630) scored 7493.00 against
7999.37 for main (39114949128). Per Prefill layer, from its profile: MegaMoE
ping-pong 1704 -> 2024 us (+19%), FA3 Prefill 492 -> 407 us, QK-norm/RoPE
prologue 152 -> 130 us, input RMSNorm + QKV quant 31.4 + 19.9 -> 46.4 us.
The clock was the same in both runs (about 1.47 GHz during MegaMoE); the
ping-pong kernel kept the Tensor Cores busy less (40% vs 44% Tensor Active),
so it is dropped together with the DeepGEMM host rebuild that selected it.

This run keeps the SGLang changes that were faster on GPU, without a
container build, through the overlay added in d863f60
(configs/firmus-framework.json sglang_overlay_patches, applied in order to
the main image a8ed4592's SGLang package and mounted over
/opt/sglang/source/python/sglang):

1. patches/sglang/add-rmsnorm-group-quant.patch: fused input RMSNorm + QKV
   group-128 FP8 quant, used only for Prefill EXTEND batches of at least
   2048 rows (Decode keeps flashinfer + group quant).
2. patches/sglang/prefill-attention-contiguous-kv.patch: vectorised Prefill
   QK-norm/RoPE/FP8 prologue that also writes contiguous FP8 K/V, and the FA3
   varlen (non-paged) path for no-prefix extends.

A third candidate, issuing the prologue's Q/K head-row and V loads before the
position -> cos/sin -> barrier chain, was byte-identical but slower in
kernel bench 39206818509 (6f9de55, 1 GPU, 3986/4027 rows: 102.9 -> 111.2 us
and 104.7 -> 113.8 us), so it is not included.

The patch files are the ones built into image b8531630 (from 19d8a61),
which passed every pre-serving gate check there; the gate script is the
19d8a61 version (check_add_rmsnorm_group_quant and check_prefill_attention),
and runs with the overlay mounted. Both patches were applied in order
to the extracted a8ed4592 SGLang tree and the JIT kernels compiled on the
Firmus login node with the image's CUDA 13 toolchain:
qknorm_rope_prefill_kernel 58 registers and add_rmsnorm_group_quant_kernel
89 registers, no spills. DeepGEMM, the Decode cap 128, concurrency 192, the
runtime switches and the image are those of main.

Expected: about -114 us per Prefill layer (about -4% Prefill time), from
the per-kernel times of 39198642036.

Validation: pytest, ruff and git diff --check pass.
